FILE: scripts/convert-to-webp.mjs
================================================================
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);
const projectRoot = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
const imagesDir = path.join(projectRoot, 'public', 'images');

console.log('--- Jardin WebP Image Conversion Script ---');
console.log('Target directory:', imagesDir);

/**
 * Utility script for image optimization and WebP conversion.
 * To use sharp for conversion, install sharp: `npm install -D sharp`
 */
async function runConversion() {
  try {
    const sharp = (await import('sharp')).default;
    
    function scanAndConvert(dir) {
      if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return;
      const files = fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true });
      for (const file of files) {
        const fullPath = path.join(dir, file.name);
        if (file.isDirectory()) {
          scanAndConvert(fullPath);
        } else if (/\.(jpe?g|png)$/i.test(file.name)) {
          const webpPath = fullPath.replace(/\.(jpe?g|png)$/i, '.webp');
          if (!fs.existsSync(webpPath)) {
            sharp(fullPath)
              .webp({ quality: 85 })
              .toFile(webpPath)
              .then(() => console.log(`Converted: ${file.name} -> WebP`))
              .catch(err => console.error(`Error converting ${file.name}:`, err.message));
          }
        }
      }
    }

    scanAndConvert(imagesDir);
    console.log('WebP optimization complete.');
  } catch {
    console.log('Sharp is not installed. To convert images automatically:');
    console.log('Run: npm install -D sharp && node scripts/convert-to-webp.mjs');
  }
}

runConversion();

FILE: scripts/generate-gallery.mjs
================================================================
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);
const projectRoot = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
const worksDir = path.join(projectRoot, 'public', 'images', 'works');
const galleryDataFile = path.join(projectRoot, 'src', 'data', 'gallery.ts');

console.log('--- Jardin Auto Gallery Generator ---');

if (!fs.existsSync(worksDir)) {
  fs.mkdirSync(worksDir, { recursive: true });
}

const files = fs.readdirSync(worksDir).filter(f => /\.(jpe?g|png|webp)$/i.test(f));

console.log(`Found ${files.length} images in public/images/works`);

const items = files.map((file, idx) => {
  const id = `work-${idx + 1}`;
  const titleRu = `Работа №${idx + 1}`;
  const titleEn = `Artwork #${idx + 1}`;
  const titleEs = `Obra #${idx + 1}`;
  return `  {
    id: '${id}',
    imageUrl: '/images/works/${file}',
    title: {
      ru: '${titleRu}',
      en: '${titleEn}',
      es: '${titleEs}',
    },
    category: 'custom',
    description: {
      ru: 'Индивидуальное постижерное изделие ручной работы.',
      en: 'Custom handcrafted postiche hairpiece.',
      es: 'Pieza de posticería artesanal personalizada.',
    },
  }`;
});

const content = `import { GalleryItem } from '../types';

export const galleryItems: GalleryItem[] = [
${items.join(',\n')}
];
`;

if (files.length > 0) {
  fs.writeFileSync(galleryDataFile, content, 'utf-8');
  console.log(`Updated ${galleryDataFile} with ${files.length} items.`);
} else {
  console.log('No images found in public/images/works to auto-generate. Keeping existing gallery.');
}

FILE: src/App.tsx
================================================================
// src/App.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { PageType } from './types';
import { LanguageProvider } from './context/LanguageContext';
import { CartProvider } from './context/CartContext';
import { ModalProvider } from './context/ModalContext';
import { Header } from './components/layout/Header';
import { Footer } from './components/layout/Footer';
import { ScrollToTopButton } from './components/layout/ScrollToTop';
import { CartModal } from './components/ui/CartModal';
import { PhotoModal } from './components/ui/PhotoModal';
import { RequestFormModal } from './components/ui/RequestFormModal';
import { ContactChoiceModal } from './components/ui/ContactChoiceModal';

import { HomePage } from './pages/HomePage';
import { GalleryPage } from './pages/GalleryPage';
import { AboutPage } from './pages/AboutPage';
import { ContactsPage } from './pages/ContactsPage';
import { PrivacyPolicyPage } from './pages/PrivacyPolicyPage';
import { CalculatorPage } from './pages/CalculatorPage';

const VALID_PAGES: PageType[] = [
  'home',
  'gallery',
  'about',
  'contacts',
  'privacy',
  'calculator',
];

const AppContent: React.FC = () => {
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState<PageType>(() => {
    const hash = window.location.hash.replace('#', '').toLowerCase();
    return VALID_PAGES.includes(hash as PageType) ? (hash as PageType) : 'home';
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleHashChange = () => {
      const hash = window.location.hash.replace('#', '').toLowerCase();
      if (VALID_PAGES.includes(hash as PageType)) {
        setCurrentPage(hash as PageType);
      } else if (!hash) {
        setCurrentPage('home');
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('hashchange', handleHashChange);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('hashchange', handleHashChange);
  }, []);

  const handleNavigate = (page: PageType) => {
    setCurrentPage(page);
    window.location.hash = page === 'home' ? '' : page;
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  return (
    <div className="app-root">
      <Header activePage={currentPage} onNavigate={handleNavigate} />

      <main className="app-main">
        {currentPage === 'home' && <HomePage onNavigate={handleNavigate} />}
        {currentPage === 'gallery' && <GalleryPage onNavigate={handleNavigate} />}
        {currentPage === 'about' && <AboutPage onNavigate={handleNavigate} />}
        {currentPage === 'contacts' && <ContactsPage onNavigate={handleNavigate} />}
        {currentPage === 'privacy' && <PrivacyPolicyPage onNavigate={handleNavigate} />}
        {currentPage === 'calculator' && <CalculatorPage onNavigate={handleNavigate} />}
      </main>

      <Footer onNavigate={handleNavigate} />

      <ScrollToTopButton />
      <CartModal onNavigateToPrivacy={() => handleNavigate('privacy')} />
      <PhotoModal
        onOrderSimilar={() => {
          handleNavigate('calculator');
        }}
      />
      <RequestFormModal onNavigateToPrivacy={() => handleNavigate('privacy')} />
      <ContactChoiceModal />
    </div>
  );
};

export const App: React.FC = () => {
  return (
    <LanguageProvider>
      <CartProvider>
        <ModalProvider>
          <AppContent />
        </ModalProvider>
      </CartProvider>
    </LanguageProvider>
  );
};

export default App;

// src/components/layout/Footer/Footer.tsx

import React from 'react';
import './Footer.css';
import { PageType } from '../../../types';
import { useLanguage } from '../../../context/LanguageContext';
import { contactInfo } from '../../../data/contacts';
import { Icon } from '../../ui/Icon';

export interface FooterProps {
  onNavigate: (page: PageType) => void;
}

export const Footer: React.FC<FooterProps> = ({ onNavigate }) => {
  const { language, t } = useLanguage();

  const handleNav = (page: PageType) => {
    onNavigate(page);
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  return (
    <footer className="site-footer">
      <div className="container">
        <div className="footer__grid">
          {/* ============================================
              BRAND
              ============================================ */}
          <div className="footer__col-brand">

              type="button"

                src="/images/logo.svg"
                alt="Jardin"
                className="footer__logo-img"
              />
            </button>

                <Icon name="telegram" size={18} />
              </a>

                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                className="footer__social-btn"
                aria-label="WhatsApp"

                <Icon name="whatsapp" size={18} />
              </a>
              <a
                href={contactInfo.instagram}
                target="_blank"

          </div>

          {/* ============================================
              NAVIGATION
              ============================================ */}
          <div>
            <h4 className="footer__col-title">{t.footer.navTitle}</h4>
            <ul className="footer__links-list">
              <li className="footer__link-item">
                <button type="button" onClick={() => handleNav('home')}>
                  {t.nav.home}
                </button>
              </li>
              <li className="footer__link-item">
                <button type="button" onClick={() => handleNav('about')}>
                  {t.nav.about}
                </button>
              </li>
              <li className="footer__link-item">
                <button type="button" onClick={() => handleNav('gallery')}>
                  {t.nav.gallery}
                </button>
              </li>
              <li className="footer__link-item">
                <button type="button" onClick={() => handleNav('contacts')}>
                  {t.nav.contacts}
                </button>
              </li>
              <li className="footer__link-item">
                <button type="button" onClick={() => handleNav('calculator')}>
                  {t.nav.calculator}
                </button>
              </li>
            </ul>
          </div>

          {/* ============================================
              CONTACTS
              ============================================ */}
          <div>
            <h4 className="footer__col-title">{t.footer.contactsTitle}</h4>
            <div className="footer__contacts-list">
              <div className="footer__contact-row">
                <span className="footer__contact-icon">
                  <Icon name="phone" size={16} />
                </span>
                <a href={`tel:${contactInfo.phoneRaw}`}>
                  {contactInfo.phone}
                </a>
              </div>
              <div className="footer__contact-row">
                <span className="footer__contact-icon">
                  <Icon name="email" size={16} />
                </span>
                <a href={`mailto:${contactInfo.email}`}>
                  {contactInfo.email}
                </a>
              </div>
              <div className="footer__contact-row">
                <span className="footer__contact-icon">
                  <Icon name="mapPin" size={16} />
                </span>
                <span>{contactInfo.address[language]}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="footer__contact-row">
                <span className="footer__contact-icon">
                  <Icon name="clock" size={16} />
                </span>
                <span>{contactInfo.workingHours[language]}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ============================================
            BOTTOM
            ============================================ */}
        <div className="footer__bottom">
          <div className="footer__copyright">{t.footer.copyright}</div>
          <div className="footer__legal-links">
            <button type="button" onClick={() => handleNav('privacy')}>
              {t.footer.privacyPolicy}
            </button>
            <span>{t.footer.madeWithLove}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </footer>
  );
};

FILE: src/components/layout/Header/Header.tsx
================================================================
// src/components/layout/Header/Header.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import './Header.css';
import { PageType, Language } from '../../../types';
import { useLanguage } from '../../../context/LanguageContext';
import { useCart } from '../../../context/CartContext';
import { Icon } from '../../ui/Icon';
import { cn } from '../../../utils/cn';

export interface HeaderProps {
  activePage: PageType;
  onNavigate: (page: PageType) => void;
}

export const Header: React.FC<HeaderProps> = ({ activePage, onNavigate }) => {
  const { language, setLanguage, t } = useLanguage();
  const { totalCount, openCart } = useCart();
  const [isScrolled, setIsScrolled] = useState(false);
  const [mobileMenuOpen, setMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleScroll = () => {
      setIsScrolled(window.scrollY > 20);
    };

    window.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll, { passive: true });
    return () => window.removeEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
  }, []);

  const handleNavClick = (page: PageType) => {
    onNavigate(page);
    setMobileMenuOpen(false);
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  const navItems: { page: PageType; label: string }[] = [
    { page: 'home', label: t.nav.home },
    { page: 'about', label: t.nav.about },
    { page: 'gallery', label: t.nav.gallery },
    { page: 'contacts', label: t.nav.contacts },
    { page: 'calculator', label: t.nav.calculator },
  ];

  return (
    <header className={cn('site-header', isScrolled && 'site-header--scrolled')}>
      <div className="container site-header__container">
        <div
          className="header__logo-container"
          onClick={() => handleNavClick('home')}
          role="button"
          tabIndex={0}
          onKeyDown={(e) => {
            if (e.key === 'Enter') handleNavClick('home');
          }}
        >
          <img src="/images/logo.svg" alt="Jardin" className="header__logo-img" />
        </div>

        <nav className="header__nav" aria-label="Main navigation">
          {navItems.map((item) => (
            <button
              key={item.page}
              type="button"
              className={cn(
                'header__nav-link',
                activePage === item.page && 'header__nav-link--active'
              )}
              onClick={() => handleNavClick(item.page)}
            >
              {item.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </nav>

        <div className="header__actions">
          <div className="header__lang-switcher" role="group" aria-label="Language selection">
            {(['ru', 'en', 'es'] as Language[]).map((lang) => (
              <button
                key={lang}
                type="button"
                className={cn(
                  'header__lang-btn',
                  language === lang && 'header__lang-btn--active'
                )}
                onClick={() => setLanguage(lang)}
              >
                {lang.toUpperCase()}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            className="header__cart-btn"
            onClick={openCart}
            aria-label={`${t.nav.cart} (${totalCount})`}
          >
            <Icon name="cart" size={20} />
            {totalCount > 0 && <span className="header__cart-badge">{totalCount}</span>}
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            className="header__mobile-toggle"
            onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen((prev) => !prev)}
            aria-label={mobileMenuOpen ? t.nav.close : t.nav.menu}
          >
            <Icon name={mobileMenuOpen ? 'close' : 'menu'} size={24} />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div
        className={cn(
          'header__mobile-drawer',
          mobileMenuOpen && 'header__mobile-drawer--open'
        )}
      >
        {navItems.map((item) => (
          <button
            key={item.page}
            type="button"
            className={cn(
              'header__mobile-link',
              activePage === item.page && 'header__mobile-link--active'
            )}
            onClick={() => handleNavClick(item.page)}
          >
            {item.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </header>
  );
};

FILE: src/components/layout/ScrollToTop/ScrollToTopButton.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import './ScrollToTopButton.css';
import { Icon } from '../../ui/Icon';
import { cn } from '../../../utils/cn';

export const ScrollToTopButton: React.FC = () => {
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleScroll = () => {
      setIsVisible(window.scrollY > 350);
    };

    window.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll, { passive: true });
    return () => window.removeEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
  }, []);

  const scrollToTop = () => {
    window.scrollTo({
      top: 0,
      behavior: 'smooth',
    });
  };

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      className={cn('scroll-to-top-btn', isVisible && 'scroll-to-top-btn--visible')}
      onClick={scrollToTop}
      aria-label="Наверх"
    >
      <Icon name="arrowUp" size={20} />
    </button>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import './Advantages.css';
import { advantages } from '../../../data/advantages';
import { useLanguage } from '../../../context/LanguageContext';
import { SectionHeader } from '../../ui/SectionHeader';
import { AdvantageCard } from './AdvantageCard';

export const AdvantagesSection: React.FC = () => {
  const { t } = useLanguage();

  return (
    <section className="section advantages-section" id="advantages">
      <div className="container">
        <SectionHeader
          badge="Преимущества"
          badgeVariant="dark"
          subtitleVariant="white"
          title={t.advantages.title}
          subtitle={t.advantages.subtitle}
        />

        <div className="advantages-grid">
          {advantages.map((item) => (
            <AdvantageCard key={item.id} advantage={item} />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

// src/components/sections/CalculatorTeaser/CalculatorTeaser.tsx

import React from 'react';
import './CalculatorTeaser.css';
import { useLanguage } from '../../../context/LanguageContext';
import { SectionHeader } from '../../ui/SectionHeader';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';
import { Icon } from '../../ui/Icon';

export interface CalculatorTeaserProps {
    onOpenCalculator: () => void;
    className?: string;
}

export const CalculatorTeaser: React.FC<CalculatorTeaserProps> = ({
    onOpenCalculator,
    className = '',
}) => {
    const { t } = useLanguage();

    const tips = [
        {
            id: 'shape',
            icon: 'crown' as const,
            title: t.calculatorTips.shapeTitle,
            text: t.calculatorTips.shapeText,
        },
        {
            id: 'length',
            icon: 'feather' as const,
            title: t.calculatorTips.lengthTitle,
            text: t.calculatorTips.lengthText,
        },
        {
            id: 'color',
            icon: 'sparkles' as const,
            title: t.calculatorTips.colorTitle,
            text: t.calculatorTips.colorText,
        },
        {
            id: 'care',
            icon: 'shield' as const,
            title: t.calculatorTips.careTitle,
            text: t.calculatorTips.careText,
        },
    ];

    return (
        <section
            className={`section calculator-teaser-section ${className}`}
            id="calculator-teaser"
        >
            <div className="container">
                <SectionHeader
                    badge={t.calculatorTips.badge}
                    title={t.calculatorTips.title}
                    subtitle={t.calculatorTips.subtitle}
                />

                <div className="calculator-teaser__grid">
                    {tips.map((tip) => (
                        <div key={tip.id} className="calculator-teaser__card">
                            <div className="calculator-teaser__icon-box">
                                <Icon name={tip.icon} size={26} />
                            </div>
                            <h3 className="calculator-teaser__card-title">{tip.title}</h3>
                            <p className="calculator-teaser__card-text">{tip.text}</p>
                        </div>
                    ))}
                </div>

                <div className="calculator-teaser__actions">
                    <Button
                        variant="primary"
                        size="lg"
                        onClick={onOpenCalculator}
                        icon={<Icon name="sparkles" size={18} className="btn-sparkle-icon" />}
                    >
                        {t.calculatorTips.openButton}
                    </Button>
                    <p className="calculator-teaser__note">{t.calculatorTips.note}</p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </section>
    );
};

// src/components/sections/CTA/CTABanner.tsx

import React from 'react';
import './CTABanner.css';
import { useLanguage } from '../../../context/LanguageContext';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';
import { Icon } from '../../ui/Icon';

export interface CTABannerProps {
  onOrderClick: () => void;
  onContactClick: () => void;
}

export const CTABanner: React.FC<CTABannerProps> = ({
  onOrderClick,
  onContactClick,
}) => {
  const { t } = useLanguage();

  return (
    <section className="section cta-section">
      <div className="container">
        <div className="cta-banner-card">
          <h2 className="cta-banner__title">
            <span className="cta-banner__title-gold">{t.cta.title}</span>
          </h2>
          <p className="cta-banner__subtitle">{t.cta.subtitle}</p>

          <div className="cta-banner__actions">
            <Button
              variant="primary"
              size="lg"
              onClick={onOrderClick}
              icon={<Icon name="sparkles" size={18} />}
            >
              {t.cta.buttonOrder}
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              size="lg"
              onClick={onContactClick}
            >
              {t.cta.buttonContact}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

FILE: src/components/sections/CustomOrder/CustomOrder.tsx
================================================================
// src/components/sections/CustomOrder/CustomOrder.tsx

import React, { useState, useId } from 'react';
import './CustomOrder.css';
import { productShapes } from '../../../data/services';
import { hairColors } from '../../../data/colors';
import { useLanguage } from '../../../context/LanguageContext';
import { useCart } from '../../../context/CartContext';
import { SectionHeader } from '../../ui/SectionHeader';
import { ColorPicker } from '../../ui/ColorPicker';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';
import { Icon } from '../../ui/Icon';
import { formatPrice } from '../../../utils/formatPrice';
import { cn } from '../../../utils/cn';

export interface CustomOrderProps {
  className?: string;
}

export const CustomOrder: React.FC<CustomOrderProps> = ({ className = '' }) => {
  const { language, t } = useLanguage();
  const { addCustomOrderItem } = useCart();
  const shapeSelectId = useId();

  // Calculator State
  const [selectedShapeId, setSelectedShapeId] = useState<string>('wig');
  const [selectedLength, setSelectedLength] = useState<number>(50);
  const [selectedColorId, setSelectedColorId] = useState<string>('medium-brown');
  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState<number>(1);
  const [justAdded, setJustAdded] = useState(false);

  const currentShape = productShapes.find((s) => s.id === selectedShapeId) || productShapes[0];
  const currentColor = hairColors.find((c) => c.id === selectedColorId) || hairColors[0];

  // 30cm -> 0.85, 50cm -> 1.05, 70cm -> 1.40
  const lengthMultiplier = 0.85 + ((selectedLength - 30) / 40) * 0.55;

  const calculatedPrice = Math.round(
    currentShape.basePrice * lengthMultiplier * currentColor.priceMultiplier
  );

  const handleQuantityChange = (delta: number) => {
    setQuantity((q) => Math.max(1, Math.min(99, q + delta)));
  };

  const handleAddToCart = () => {
    addCustomOrderItem({
      shapeId: currentShape.id,
      shapeName: currentShape.name,
      lengthCm: selectedLength,
      colorId: currentColor.id,
      colorName: currentColor.name,
      colorHex: currentColor.hex,
      quantity,
      unitPrice: calculatedPrice,
    });
    setJustAdded(true);
    setQuantity(1);
    setTimeout(() => setJustAdded(false), 2000);
  };

  return (
    <section className={cn('section custom-order-section', className)} id="custom-order">
      <div className="container">
        <SectionHeader
          badge={t.customOrder.badge}
          title={t.customOrder.title}
          subtitle={t.customOrder.subtitle}
        />

        <div className="custom-order-calculator">
          {/* Left Column: Configuration */}
          <div className="calc-config-col">
            {/* Step 1: Shape */}
            <div className="calc-step">
              <label htmlFor={shapeSelectId} className="calc-step__title">
                <Icon name="crown" size={18} />
                <span>{t.customOrder.step1}</span>
              </label>

              <div className="shape-select-grid">
                {productShapes.map((shape) => {
                  const isActive = shape.id === selectedShapeId;
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={shape.id}
                      className={cn('shape-option-card', isActive && 'shape-option-card--active')}
                      onClick={() => setSelectedShapeId(shape.id)}
                      role="button"
                      tabIndex={0}
                      onKeyDown={(e) => {
                        if (e.key === 'Enter') setSelectedShapeId(shape.id);
                      }}
                    >
                      <div className="shape-option-name">{shape.name[language]}</div>
                      <div className="shape-option-sub">{shape.description[language]}</div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Step 2: Length */}
            <div className="calc-step">
              <h3 className="calc-step__title">
                <Icon name="feather" size={18} />
                <span>{t.customOrder.step2}</span>
              </h3>

              <div className="length-slider-container">
                <div className="length-slider-header">
                  <span style={{ fontSize: '0.9rem', color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>
                    {t.customOrder.lengthLabel}
                  </span>
                  <span className="length-slider-value">
                    {selectedLength} {t.shop.cm}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <input
                  type="range"
                  min={30}
                  max={70}
                  step={5}
                  value={selectedLength}
                  onChange={(e) => setSelectedLength(Number(e.target.value))}
                  className="length-slider-range"
                  aria-label="Выбор длины волос в сантиметрах"
                />

                <div className="length-quick-pills">
                  {[30, 40, 50, 60, 70].map((len) => (
                    <button
                      key={len}
                      type="button"
                      className={cn(
                        'length-pill-btn',
                        selectedLength === len && 'length-pill-btn--active'
                      )}
                      onClick={() => setSelectedLength(len)}
                    >
                      {len} {t.shop.cm}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Step 3: Color */}
            <div className="calc-step">
              <h3 className="calc-step__title">
                <Icon name="sparkles" size={18} />
                <span>{t.customOrder.step3}</span>
              </h3>

              <ColorPicker
                selectedColorId={selectedColorId}
                onSelectColor={(color) => setSelectedColorId(color.id)}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Right Column: Price + Add to Cart */}
          <div className="calc-summary-col">
            <div className="calc-price-display-box">
              <div className="calc-price-label">{t.customOrder.totalPrice}</div>
              <div className="calc-price-total">
                {formatPrice(calculatedPrice, '€', language)}
              </div>
              <div className="calc-formula-note">{t.customOrder.formulaNote}</div>
            </div>

            <div className="calc-add-to-cart">
              <div className="calc-quantity-row">
                <span>{t.customOrder.quantityLabel}</span>
                <div className="calc-qty-control">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleQuantityChange(-1)}
                    aria-label="Уменьшить количество"
                  >
                    <Icon name="minus" size={14} />
                  </button>
                  <span>{quantity}</span>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleQuantityChange(1)}
                    aria-label="Увеличить количество"
                  >
                    <Icon name="plus" size={14} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <Button
                variant={justAdded ? 'outline' : 'primary'}
                size="lg"
                fullWidth
                onClick={handleAddToCart}
                icon={<Icon name={justAdded ? 'check' : 'cart'} size={18} />}
              >
                {justAdded ? t.customOrder.addedToCart : t.customOrder.addToCart}
              </Button>

              <p className="calc-add-note">{t.customOrder.addToCartNote}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

// src/components/sections/Gallery/GallerySection.tsx

import React from 'react';
import './GallerySection.css';
import { galleryItems } from '../../../data/gallery';
import { useLanguage } from '../../../context/LanguageContext';
import { useModal } from '../../../context/ModalContext';
import { SectionHeader } from '../../ui/SectionHeader';
import { GalleryItem } from '../../ui/GalleryItem';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';
import { Icon } from '../../ui/Icon';
import { cn } from '../../../utils/cn';

export interface GallerySectionProps {
  isPreview?: boolean;
  onViewAllClick?: () => void;
  className?: string;
}

export const GallerySection: React.FC<GallerySectionProps> = ({
  isPreview = false,
  onViewAllClick,
  className = '',
}) => {
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  const { openPhotoModal } = useModal();

  const displayedItems = isPreview ? galleryItems.slice(0, 3) : galleryItems;

  return (
    <section className={cn('section', className)} id="gallery">
      <div className="container">
        <SectionHeader
          badge={t.gallery.badge}
          title={t.gallery.title}
          subtitle={t.gallery.subtitle}
        />

        <div className="gallery-grid">
          {displayedItems.map((item) => (
            <GalleryItem
              key={item.id}
              item={item}
              onClick={(clicked) => openPhotoModal(clicked)}
            />
          ))}
        </div>

        {isPreview && onViewAllClick && (
          <div className="gallery-preview-footer">
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              size="lg"
              onClick={onViewAllClick}
              icon={<Icon name="arrowRight" size={18} />}
              iconPosition="right"
            >
              {t.gallery.viewMore}
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

              <span className="hero__stat-value">{t.hero.handCrafted}</span>
              <span className="hero__stat-label">Невидимый эффект естественного роста</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

FILE: src/components/ui/Button/Button.tsx

import React from 'react';
import './Button.css';
import { cn } from '../../../utils/cn';

export interface ButtonProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: 'primary' | 'outline' | 'ghost';
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
  fullWidth?: boolean;
  icon?: React.ReactNode;
  iconPosition?: 'left' | 'right';
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

export const Button: React.FC<ButtonProps> = ({
  variant = 'primary',
  size = 'md',
  fullWidth = false,
  icon,
  iconPosition = 'left',
  className = '',
  children,
  ...props
}) => {
  return (
    <button
      className={cn(
        'custom-btn',
        `custom-btn--${variant}`,
        `custom-btn--${size}`,
        fullWidth && 'custom-btn--full',
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {icon && iconPosition === 'left' && <span className="btn-icon">{icon}</span>}
      <span className="btn-text">{children}</span>
      {icon && iconPosition === 'right' && <span className="btn-icon">{icon}</span>}
    </button>
  );
};

================================================================
FILE: src/components/ui/CartModal/CartModal.tsx
================================================================
// src/components/ui/CartModal/CartModal.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import './CartModal.css';
import { Modal } from '../Modal';
import { useCart } from '../../../context/CartContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../../../context/LanguageContext';
import { formatPrice } from '../../../utils/formatPrice';
import { formatPhone, isValidPhone } from '../../../utils/formatPhone';
import { Button } from '../Button';
import { Icon } from '../Icon';
import { sendRequest } from '../../../utils/sendRequest';

export interface CartModalProps {
  onNavigateToPrivacy?: () => void;
}

type CartView = 'cart' | 'form' | 'success';

export const CartModal: React.FC<CartModalProps> = ({ onNavigateToPrivacy }) => {
  const {
    items,
    totalCount,
    totalPrice,
    isCartOpen,
    closeCart,
    updateQuantity,
    removeFromCart,
    clearCart,
  } = useCart();
  const { language, t } = useLanguage();

  const [view, setView] = useState<CartView>('cart');
  const [clientName, setClientName] = useState('');
  const [clientPhone, setClientPhone] = useState('');
  const [clientEmail, setClientEmail] = useState('');
  const [contactMethod, setContactMethod] = useState<'telegram' | 'whatsapp' | 'phone' | 'email'>('telegram');
  const [comment, setComment] = useState('');
  const [agreed, setAgreed] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);

  const handlePhoneChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    setClientPhone(formatPhone(e.target.value));
  };

  const handleGoToForm = () => {
    setError('');
    setView('form');
  };

  const handleBackToCart = () => {
    setError('');
    setView('cart');
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();

    if (!clientName.trim()) {
      setError(t.errors.requiredName);
      return;
    }
    if (!isValidPhone(clientPhone)) {
      setError(t.errors.invalidPhone);
      return;
    }
    if (clientEmail.trim() && !/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(clientEmail.trim())) {
      setError(t.errors.invalidEmail);
      return;
    }
    if (!agreed) {
      setError(t.errors.privacyRequired);
      return;
    }

    setError('');
    setIsSubmitting(true);

    const itemsSummary = items
      .map((item, idx) => {
        const subtotal = item.unitPrice * item.quantity;
        return `${idx + 1}. ${item.shapeName[language]} — ${item.lengthCm} см — ${item.colorName[language]}\n   ${item.quantity} × ${formatPrice(item.unitPrice, '€', language)} = ${formatPrice(subtotal, '€', language)}`;
      })
      .join('\n\n');

    const message = `Состав заявки (${items.length} поз., итого ${formatPrice(totalPrice, '€', language)}):\n\n${itemsSummary}`;

    const result = await sendRequest({
      name: clientName,
      phone: clientPhone,
      email: clientEmail,
      message,
      preferredMethod: contactMethod,
    });

    setIsSubmitting(false);

    if (result.success) {
      setView('success');
    } else {
      setError(result.message || t.errors.generalError);
    }
  };

  const handleClose = () => {
    if (view === 'success') {
      clearCart();
      setClientName('');
      setClientPhone('');
      setClientEmail('');
      setComment('');
      setAgreed(false);
      setView('cart');
    }
    setError('');
    closeCart();
  };

  const getModalTitle = (): string | undefined => {
    if (view === 'success') return undefined;
    if (view === 'form') return t.cart.submitRequest;
    return `${t.cart.title} (${totalCount})`;
  };

  return (
    <Modal isOpen={isCartOpen} onClose={handleClose} title={getModalTitle()} size="md">
      <div className="cart-modal-body">
        {/* SUCCESS VIEW */}
        {view === 'success' && (
          <div className="cart-order-success">
            <Icon name="check" size={56} className="cart-order-success__icon" />
            <h4 className="cart-order-success__title">
              {t.customOrder.successTitle}
            </h4>
            <p className="cart-order-success__text">
              {t.customOrder.successDesc}
            </p>
            <Button
              variant="primary"
              onClick={handleClose}
              className="cart-order-success__btn"
            >
              {t.cart.closeButton}
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* EMPTY CART */}
        {view === 'cart' && items.length === 0 && (
          <div className="cart-empty-state">
            <Icon name="cart" size={56} />
            <h4 className="cart-empty-title">{t.cart.emptyTitle}</h4>
            <p className="cart-empty-text">{t.cart.emptySubtitle}</p>
            <Button
              variant="primary"
              onClick={() => {
                closeCart();
                setTimeout(() => {
                  document.getElementById('custom-order')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
                }, 250);
              }}
            >
              {t.cart.goToCalculator}
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* CART LIST VIEW */}
        {view === 'cart' && items.length > 0 && (
          <>
            <div className="cart-items-list">
              {items.map((item) => (
                <div key={item.id} className="cart-item-row">
                  <div
                    className="cart-item-color-thumb"
                    style={{ backgroundColor: item.colorHex }}
                    aria-hidden
                  />

                  <div className="cart-item-info">
                    <h5 className="cart-item-name">{item.shapeName[language]}</h5>
                    <div className="cart-item-meta">
                      <span>{t.shop.length}: {item.lengthCm} {t.shop.cm}</span>
                      <span className="cart-item-color-meta">
                        <span className="cart-color-dot" style={{ backgroundColor: item.colorHex }} />
                        {item.colorName[language]}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="cart-item-price">
                      {formatPrice(item.unitPrice * item.quantity, '€', language)}
                      <span className="cart-item-unit-price">
                        {' '}({item.quantity} × {formatPrice(item.unitPrice, '€', language)})
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="cart-item-controls">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="cart-qty-btn"
                      onClick={() => updateQuantity(item.id, item.quantity - 1)}
                      aria-label="Уменьшить количество"
                    >
                      <Icon name="minus" size={14} />
                    </button>
                    <span className="cart-qty-val">{item.quantity}</span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="cart-qty-btn"
                      onClick={() => updateQuantity(item.id, item.quantity + 1)}
                      aria-label="Увеличить количество"
                    >
                      <Icon name="plus" size={14} />
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="cart-item-remove"
                      onClick={() => removeFromCart(item.id)}
                      aria-label="Удалить позицию"
                      title={t.cart.removeItem}
                    >
                      <Icon name="trash" size={18} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="cart-summary">
              <div className="cart-summary-row">
                <span className="cart-summary-total-label">{t.cart.totalAmount}</span>
                <span className="cart-summary-total-value">
                  {formatPrice(totalPrice, '€', language)}
                </span>
              </div>

              <Button
                variant="primary"
                size="lg"
                fullWidth
                onClick={handleGoToForm}
                icon={<Icon name="arrowRight" size={18} />}
              >
                {t.cart.submitRequest}
              </Button>

              <Button
                variant="ghost"
                size="md"
                fullWidth
                onClick={() => {
                  closeCart();
                  setTimeout(() => {
                    document.getElementById('custom-order')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
                  }, 250);
                }}
              >
                {t.cart.continueSelecting}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        {/* FORM VIEW */}
        {view === 'form' && (
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="cart-form">
            <div className="cart-form-summary">
              <span className="cart-form-summary-label">{t.cart.orderSummary}</span>
              <span className="cart-form-summary-value">
                {items.length} поз. · {formatPrice(totalPrice, '€', language)}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="cart-form-group">
              <label className="cart-form-label">{t.forms.name} *</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                required
                value={clientName}
                onChange={(e) => setClientName(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={t.forms.namePlaceholder}
                className="cart-form-input"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="cart-form-group">
              <label className="cart-form-label">{t.forms.phone} *</label>
              <input
                type="tel"
                required
                value={clientPhone}
                onChange={handlePhoneChange}
                placeholder={t.forms.phonePlaceholder}
                className="cart-form-input"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="cart-form-group">
              <label className="cart-form-label">{t.forms.email}</label>
              <input
                type="email"
                value={clientEmail}
                onChange={(e) => setClientEmail(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={t.forms.emailPlaceholder}
                className="cart-form-input"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="cart-form-group">
              <label className="cart-form-label">{t.customOrder.contactMethod}</label>
              <div className="cart-method-grid">
                {(['telegram', 'whatsapp', 'phone', 'email'] as const).map((m) => (
                  <button
                    key={m}
                    type="button"
                    className={`cart-method-btn ${contactMethod === m ? 'cart-method-btn--active' : ''}`}
                    onClick={() => setContactMethod(m)}
                  >
                    {t.customOrder.methods[m]}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="cart-form-group">
              <label className="cart-form-label">{t.cart.commentLabel}</label>
              <textarea
                rows={3}
                value={comment}
                onChange={(e) => setComment(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={t.cart.commentPlaceholder}
                className="cart-form-input cart-form-textarea"
              />
            </div>

            <label className="cart-agreement">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={agreed}
                onChange={(e) => setAgreed(e.target.checked)}
              />
              <span>
                {t.customOrder.privacyNotice}{' '}
                {onNavigateToPrivacy && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="cart-privacy-link"
                    onClick={(e) => {
                      e.preventDefault();
                      closeCart();
                      onNavigateToPrivacy();
                    }}
                  >
                    {t.customOrder.privacyLink}
                  </button>
                )}
              </span>
            </label>

            {error && <div className="cart-error-msg">{error}</div>}

            <div className="cart-form-actions">
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="ghost"
                onClick={handleBackToCart}
                disabled={isSubmitting}
              >
                {t.cart.backToCart}
              </Button>
              <Button
                type="submit"
                variant="primary"
                disabled={isSubmitting}
                icon={<Icon name="check" size={18} />}
              >
                {isSubmitting ? t.customOrder.sending : t.cart.submitRequest}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

FILE: src/components/ui/ColorPicker/ColorPicker.tsx

import React from 'react';
import './ColorPicker.css';
import { HairColor } from '../../../types';
import { hairColors } from '../../../data/colors';
import { useLanguage } from '../../../context/LanguageContext';
import { cn } from '../../../utils/cn';

export interface ColorPickerProps {
  selectedColorId: string;
  onSelectColor: (color: HairColor) => void;
  className?: string;
}

export const ColorPicker: React.FC<ColorPickerProps> = ({
  selectedColorId,
  onSelectColor,
  className = '',
}) => {
  const { language, t } = useLanguage();

  const activeColor = hairColors.find((c) => c.id === selectedColorId) || hairColors[0];

  return (
    <div className={cn('color-picker-wrap', className)}>
      <div className="color-swatches-grid" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Палитра оттенков">
        {hairColors.map((color) => {
          const isSelected = color.id === selectedColorId;
          const localizedName = color.name[language];

          return (
            <button
              key={color.id}
              type="button"
              role="radio"
              aria-checked={isSelected}
              className={cn('color-swatch-item', isSelected && 'color-swatch-item--active')}
              onClick={() => onSelectColor(color)}
              title={`${localizedName} (${color.priceMultiplier > 1 ? `+${Math.round((color.priceMultiplier - 1) * 100)}%` : 'Стандарт'})`}
            >
              <span
                className="color-dot-indicator"
                style={{ backgroundColor: color.hex }}
              />
              <div className="color-meta-info">
                <span className="color-name-label">{localizedName}</span>
                <span className="color-multiplier-tag">
                  {color.priceMultiplier > 1
                    ? `+${Math.round((color.priceMultiplier - 1) * 100)}% к базе`
                    : 'Базовая цена'}
                </span>
              </div>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {activeColor && (
        <div className="color-selected-preview">
          <div className="color-preview-left">
            <span
              className="color-preview-circle"
              style={{ backgroundColor: activeColor.hex }}
            />
            <span className="color-preview-text">
              {t.customOrder.colorSelected} <strong>{activeColor.name[language]}</strong>
            </span>
          </div>
          <span className="color-preview-badge">
            Коэф. {activeColor.priceMultiplier.toFixed(2)}x
          </span>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import './ContactChoiceModal.css';
import { Modal } from '../Modal';
import { useModal } from '../../../context/ModalContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../../../context/LanguageContext';
import { contactInfo } from '../../../data/contacts';
import { Icon } from '../Icon';

export const ContactChoiceModal: React.FC = () => {
  const { isContactChoiceOpen, closeContactChoiceModal, contactChoiceData } = useModal();
  const { t } = useLanguage();

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isContactChoiceOpen}
      onClose={closeContactChoiceModal}
      title={contactChoiceData?.title || t.contacts.messengersTitle}
      size="md"
    >
      <div className="contact-choice-list">
        <a
          href={contactInfo.telegram}
          target="_blank"
          rel="noopener noreferrer"
          className="contact-choice-item"
          onClick={() => {
            contactChoiceData?.onSelectMethod?.('telegram');
            closeContactChoiceModal();
          }}
        >
          <div className="contact-choice-info">
            <span className="contact-choice-icon">
              <Icon name="telegram" size={24} />
            </span>
            <div>
              <span className="contact-choice-title">Telegram</span>
              <span className="contact-choice-sub">Быстрый ответ мастера</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <Icon name="arrowRight" size={18} />
        </a>

        <a
          href={contactInfo.whatsapp}
          target="_blank"
          rel="noopener noreferrer"
          className="contact-choice-item"
          onClick={() => {
            contactChoiceData?.onSelectMethod?.('whatsapp');
            closeContactChoiceModal();
          }}
        >
          <div className="contact-choice-info">
            <span className="contact-choice-icon">
              <Icon name="whatsapp" size={24} />
            </span>
            <div>
              <span className="contact-choice-title">WhatsApp</span>
              <span className="contact-choice-sub">Консультация и отправка фото</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <Icon name="arrowRight" size={18} />
        </a>

        <a
          href={`tel:${contactInfo.phoneRaw}`}
          className="contact-choice-item"
          onClick={() => {
            contactChoiceData?.onSelectMethod?.('phone');
            closeContactChoiceModal();
          }}
        >
          <div className="contact-choice-info">
            <span className="contact-choice-icon">
              <Icon name="phone" size={24} />
            </span>
            <div>
              <span className="contact-choice-title">{contactInfo.phone}</span>
              <span className="contact-choice-sub">Прямой звонок в ателье</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <Icon name="arrowRight" size={18} />
        </a>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

FILE: src/components/ui/GalleryItem/GalleryItem.tsx
================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import './GalleryItem.css';
import { GalleryItem as GalleryItemType } from '../../../types';
import { useLanguage } from '../../../context/LanguageContext';
import { Icon } from '../Icon';
import { cn } from '../../../utils/cn';

export interface GalleryItemProps {
  item: GalleryItemType;
  onClick: (item: GalleryItemType) => void;
  className?: string;
}

export const GalleryItem: React.FC<GalleryItemProps> = ({
  item,
  onClick,
  className = '',
}) => {
  const { language } = useLanguage();
  const [hasError, setHasError] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div
      className={cn('gallery-card', className)}
      onClick={() => onClick(item)}
      role="button"
      tabIndex={0}
      onKeyDown={(e) => {
        if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
          onClick(item);
        }
      }}
    >
      {!hasError ? (
        <img
          src={item.imageUrl}
          alt={item.title[language]}
          className="gallery-card__img"
          loading="lazy"
          onError={() => setHasError(true)}
        />
      ) : (
        <div
          style={{
            width: '100%',
            height: '100%',
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            background: 'radial-gradient(circle, #222 0%, #0d0d0d 100%)',
            color: 'var(--color-gold)',
          }}
        >
          <Icon name="sparkles" size={36} />
        </div>
      )}

      <button
        type="button"
        className="gallery-card__zoom-btn"
        aria-label="Увеличить фото"
        onClick={(e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          onClick(item);
        }}
      >
        <Icon name="zoom" size={16} />
      </button>

      <div className="gallery-card__overlay">
        <h4 className="gallery-card__title">{item.title[language]}</h4>
        <p className="gallery-card__desc">{item.description[language]}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src/components/ui/Icon/Icon.tsx
================================================================
import React from 'react';
import { IconName, iconPaths } from './icons';

export interface IconProps extends React.SVGProps<SVGSVGElement> {
  name: IconName;
  size?: number | string;
  className?: string;
}

export const Icon: React.FC<IconProps> = ({ name, size = 20, className = '', ...props }) => {
  const IconComponent = iconPaths[name];

  if (!IconComponent) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <span
      className={`icon-wrapper ${className}`}
      style={{
        display: 'inline-flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center',
        width: size,
        height: size,
        flexShrink: 0,
      }}
    >
      <IconComponent width={size} height={size} {...props} />
    </span>
  );
};

import React from 'react';

export type IconName =
  | 'cart'
  | 'close'
  | 'check'
  | 'plus'
  | 'minus'
  | 'trash'
  | 'menu'
  | 'phone'
  | 'email'
  | 'mapPin'
  | 'clock'
  | 'telegram'
  | 'whatsapp'
  | 'instagram'
  | 'tiktok'
  | 'diamond'
  | 'feather'
  | 'crown'
  | 'sparkles'
  | 'shield'
  | 'certificate'
  | 'star'
  | 'arrowRight'
  | 'arrowUp'
  | 'zoom';

export const iconPaths: Record<IconName, (props: React.SVGProps<SVGSVGElement>) => React.ReactElement> = {
  cart: (props) => (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.8" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" {...props}>
      <circle cx="9" cy="21" r="1" />
      <circle cx="20" cy="21" r="1" />
      <path d="M1 1h4l2.68 13.39a2 2 0 0 0 2 1.61h9.72a2 2 0 0 0 2-1.61L23 6H6" />
    </svg>
  ),
  close: (props) => (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" {...props}>
      <line x1="18" y1="6" x2="6" y2="18" />
      <line x1="6" y1="6" x2="18" y2="18" />
    </svg>
  ),
  check: (props) => (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" {...props}>
      <polyline points="20 6 9 17 4 12" />
    </svg>
  ),
  plus: (props) => (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" {...props}>
      <line x1="12" y1="5" x2="12" y2="19" />
      <line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12" />
    </svg>
  ),
  minus: (props) => (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" {...props}>
      <line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12" />
    </svg>
  ),
  trash: (props) => (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.8" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" {...props}>
      <polyline points="3 6 5 6 21 6" />
      <path d="M19 6v14a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H7a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V6m3 0V4a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h4a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v2" />
    </svg>
  ),
  menu: (props) => (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" {...props}>
      <line x1="3" y1="12" x2="21" y2="12" />
      <line x1="3" y1="6" x2="21" y2="6" />
      <line x1="3" y1="18" x2="21" y2="18" />
    </svg>
  ),
  phone: (props) => (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.8" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" {...props}>
      <path d="M22 16.92v3a2 2 0 0 1-2.18 2 19.79 19.79 0 0 1-8.63-3.07 19.5 19.5 0 0 1-6-6 19.79 19.79 0 0 1-3.07-8.67A2 2 0 0 1 4.11 2h3a2 2 0 0 1 2 1.72 12.84 12.84 0 0 0 .7 2.81 2 2 0 0 1-.45 2.11L8.09 9.91a16 16 0 0 0 6 6l1.27-1.27a2 2 0 0 1 2.11-.45 12.84 12.84 0 0 0 2.81.7A2 2 0 0 1 22 16.92z" />
    </svg>
  ),
  email: (props) => (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.8" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" {...props}>
      <path d="M4 4h16c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2v12c0 1.1-.9 2-2 2H4c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V6c0-1.1.9-2 2-2z" />
      <polyline points="22,6 12,13 2,6" />
    </svg>
  ),

FILE: src/components/ui/Modal/Modal.tsx
================================================================
// src/components/ui/Modal/Modal.tsx

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import './Modal.css';
import { Icon } from '../Icon';
import { cn } from '../../../utils/cn';

export interface ModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title?: string;
  size?: 'md' | 'lg';
  children: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}

export const Modal: React.FC<ModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  title,
  size = 'md',
  children,
  className = '',
}) => {
  const overlayRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        onClose();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    const originalOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';

    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
      document.body.style.overflow = originalOverflow;
    };
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleOverlayClick = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (e.target === overlayRef.current) {
      onClose();
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      ref={overlayRef}
      className="app-modal-overlay"
      onClick={handleOverlayClick}
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-label={title || 'Modal window'}
    >
      <div
        className={cn(
          'app-modal-container',
          size === 'lg' && 'app-modal-container--lg',
          className
        )}
      >
        {title && (
          <div className="app-modal-header">
            <h3 className="app-modal-title">{title}</h3>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="app-modal-close"
              onClick={onClose}
              aria-label="Закрыть"
            >
              <Icon name="close" size={18} />
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {!title && (
          <button
            type="button"
            className="app-modal-close"
            style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 16, right: 16, zIndex: 10 }}
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Закрыть"
          >
            <Icon name="close" size={18} />
          </button>
        )}

        <div className="app-modal-content">{children}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src/components/ui/PhotoModal/PhotoModal.tsx
================================================================
import React from 'react';
import './PhotoModal.css';
import { Modal } from '../Modal';
import { useModal } from '../../../context/ModalContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../../../context/LanguageContext';
import { Button } from '../Button';
import { Icon } from '../Icon';

export interface PhotoModalProps {
  onOrderSimilar?: (title: string) => void;
}

export const PhotoModal: React.FC<PhotoModalProps> = ({ onOrderSimilar }) => {
  const { activePhoto, closePhotoModal } = useModal();
  const { language, t } = useLanguage();

  if (!activePhoto) return null;

  const handleOrder = () => {
    const titleText = activePhoto.title[language];
    closePhotoModal();
    if (onOrderSimilar) {
      onOrderSimilar(titleText);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={Boolean(activePhoto)}
      onClose={closePhotoModal}
      size="lg"
      title={activePhoto.title[language]}
    >
      <div className="photo-modal-wrap">
        <div className="photo-modal-preview-box">
          <img
            src={activePhoto.imageUrl}
            alt={activePhoto.title[language]}
            className="photo-modal-img"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="photo-modal-info">
          <div className="photo-modal-text-box">
            <h4 className="photo-modal-caption-title">{activePhoto.title[language]}</h4>
            <p className="photo-modal-caption-desc">{activePhoto.description[language]}</p>
          </div>

          <Button
            variant="primary"
            size="md"
            onClick={handleOrder}
            icon={<Icon name="sparkles" size={16} />}
          >
            {t.gallery.requestSimilar}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

// src/components/ui/RequestFormModal/RequestFormModal.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import './RequestFormModal.css';
import { Modal } from '../Modal';
import { useModal } from '../../../context/ModalContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../../../context/LanguageContext';
import { Button } from '../Button';
import { Icon } from '../Icon';
import { formatPhone } from '../../../utils/formatPhone';
import { sendRequest } from '../../../utils/sendRequest';

export interface RequestFormModalProps {
  onNavigateToPrivacy?: () => void;
}

export const RequestFormModal: React.FC<RequestFormModalProps> = ({ onNavigateToPrivacy }) => {
  const { isRequestModalOpen, requestModalTitle, closeRequestModal } = useModal();
  const { t } = useLanguage();

  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
  const [method, setMethod] = useState<'telegram' | 'whatsapp' | 'phone'>('telegram');
  const [agreed, setAgreed] = useState(false);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [isSuccess, setIsSuccess] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState('');

  const handlePhoneChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    setPhone(formatPhone(e.target.value));
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!agreed) {
      setErrorMsg(t.errors.privacyRequired);
      return;
    }
    setErrorMsg('');
    setIsSubmitting(true);

    const res = await sendRequest({
      name,
      phone,
      email,
      message: `${requestModalTitle ? `[Тема: ${requestModalTitle}] ` : ''}${message}`,
      preferredMethod: method,
    });

    setIsSubmitting(false);
    if (res.success) {
      setIsSuccess(true);
      setName('');
      setPhone('');
      setEmail('');
      setMessage('');
    } else {
      setErrorMsg(t.errors.generalError);
    }
  };

  const handleClose = () => {
    setIsSuccess(false);
    setErrorMsg('');
    closeRequestModal();
  };

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isRequestModalOpen}
      onClose={handleClose}
      title={requestModalTitle || t.contacts.formTitle}
      size="md"
    >
      {isSuccess ? (
        <div className="request-success">
          <Icon name="check" size={56} className="request-success__icon" />
          <h4 className="request-success__title">
            {t.customOrder.successTitle}
          </h4>
          <p className="request-success__text">
            {t.customOrder.successDesc}
          </p>
          <Button
            variant="primary"
            onClick={handleClose}
            className="request-success__btn"
          >
            {t.cart.closeButton}
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="request-form">
          <div className="request-form__group">
            <label className="request-form__label">
              {t.forms.name} *
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={t.forms.namePlaceholder}
              className="request-form__input"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="request-form__group">
            <label className="request-form__label">
              {t.forms.phone} *
            </label>
            <input
              type="tel"
              required
              value={phone}
              onChange={handlePhoneChange}
              placeholder={t.forms.phonePlaceholder}
              className="request-form__input"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="request-form__group">
            <label className="request-form__label">
              {t.forms.email}
            </label>
            <input
              type="email"
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={t.forms.emailPlaceholder}
              className="request-form__input"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="request-form__group">
            <label className="request-form__label">
              {t.customOrder.contactMethod}
            </label>
            <div className="request-form__method-grid">
              {(['telegram', 'whatsapp', 'phone'] as const).map((m) => (
                <button
                  key={m}
                  type="button"
                  className={`request-form__method-btn ${method === m ? 'request-form__method-btn--active' : ''
                    }`}
                  onClick={() => setMethod(m)}
                >
                  {t.customOrder.methods[m]}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="request-form__group">
            <label className="request-form__label">
              {t.forms.message}
            </label>
            <textarea
              rows={3}
              value={message}
              onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={t.forms.messagePlaceholder}
              className="request-form__input request-form__textarea"
            />
          </div>

          <label className="request-form__agreement">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              required
              checked={agreed}
              onChange={(e) => setAgreed(e.target.checked)}
              className="request-form__checkbox"
            />
            <span>
              {t.customOrder.privacyNotice}{' '}
              {onNavigateToPrivacy ? (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="request-form__privacy-link"
                  onClick={(e) => {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    handleClose();
                    onNavigateToPrivacy();
                  }}
                >
                  {t.customOrder.privacyLink}
                </button>
              ) : (
                <span className="request-form__privacy-link">
                  {t.customOrder.privacyLink}
                </span>
              )}
            </span>
          </label>

          {errorMsg && (
            <div className="request-form__error">{errorMsg}</div>
          )}

          <Button
            type="submit"
            variant="primary"
            fullWidth
            disabled={isSubmitting}
            icon={<Icon name="sparkles" size={18} className="btn-sparkle-icon" />}
          >
            {isSubmitting ? t.customOrder.sending : t.forms.send}
          </Button>
        </form>
      )}
    </Modal>
  );
};

// src/components/ui/SectionHeader/SectionHeader.tsx

import React from 'react';
import './SectionHeader.css';
import { Icon } from '../Icon';
import { cn } from '../../../utils/cn';

export interface SectionHeaderProps {
  badge?: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  align?: 'center' | 'left';
  className?: string;
  /** 'light' — светлая 3D-капсула (как hero__badge), 'dark' — тёмная 3D-капсула со звёздочками */
  badgeVariant?: 'light' | 'dark';
  /** Вариант цвета описания: 'default' — приглушённый, 'white' — белый */
  subtitleVariant?: 'default' | 'white';
}

export const SectionHeader: React.FC<SectionHeaderProps> = ({
  badge,
  title,
  subtitle,
  align = 'center',
  className = '',
  badgeVariant = 'light',
  subtitleVariant = 'default',
}) => {
  const isDark = badgeVariant === 'dark';
  const isSubtitleWhite = subtitleVariant === 'white';

  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        'section-header-wrap',
        align === 'left' && 'section-header-wrap--left',
        className
      )}
    >
      {badge && (
        <div
          className={cn(
            'capsule',
            isDark && 'capsule--dark',
            'section-header-tag',
            isDark && 'section-header-tag--dark'
          )}
        >
          {isDark && (
            <Icon
              name="star"
              size={14}
              className="section-header-tag__star section-header-tag__star--left"
            />
          )}
          <span className="section-header-tag__text">{badge}</span>
          {isDark && (
            <Icon
              name="star"
              size={14}
              className="section-header-tag__star section-header-tag__star--right"
            />
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      <h2 className="section-header-title">
        <span className="gold-accent">{title}</span>
      </h2>

      {subtitle && (
        <p
          className={cn(
            'section-header-desc',
            isSubtitleWhite && 'section-header-desc-white'
          )}
        >
          {subtitle}
        </p>
      )}

      <div className="section-header-line" />
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src/context/CartContext.tsx
