// src/context/CartContext.tsx

import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, ReactNode } from 'react';
import { CustomOrderCartItem } from '../types';

interface CartContextType {
  items: CustomOrderCartItem[];
  addCustomOrderItem: (item: Omit<CustomOrderCartItem, 'id'>) => void;
  removeFromCart: (itemId: string) => void;
  updateQuantity: (itemId: string, quantity: number) => void;
  clearCart: () => void;
  totalCount: number;
  totalPrice: number;
  isCartOpen: boolean;
  openCart: () => void;
  closeCart: () => void;
}

const CartContext = createContext<CartContextType | undefined>(undefined);

const CART_STORAGE_KEY = 'jardin_custom_order_cart_v2';

/**
 * Валидация позиции корзины, загруженной из localStorage.
 * Защищает приложение от падения, если в хранилище лежат
 * данные старого/повреждённого формата.
 */
function isValidCartItem(item: unknown): item is CustomOrderCartItem {
  if (!item || typeof item !== 'object') return false;

  const it = item as Record<string, unknown>;

  return (
    typeof it.id === 'string' &&
    typeof it.shapeId === 'string' &&
    it.shapeName !== null &&
    typeof it.shapeName === 'object' &&
    typeof (it.shapeName as Record<string, unknown>).ru === 'string' &&
    typeof (it.shapeName as Record<string, unknown>).en === 'string' &&
    typeof (it.shapeName as Record<string, unknown>).es === 'string' &&
    typeof it.lengthCm === 'number' &&
    typeof it.colorId === 'string' &&
    it.colorName !== null &&
    typeof it.colorName === 'object' &&
    typeof (it.colorName as Record<string, unknown>).ru === 'string' &&
    typeof (it.colorName as Record<string, unknown>).en === 'string' &&
    typeof (it.colorName as Record<string, unknown>).es === 'string' &&
    typeof it.colorHex === 'string' &&
    typeof it.quantity === 'number' &&
    typeof it.unitPrice === 'number'
  );
}

function loadCartFromStorage(): CustomOrderCartItem[] {
  try {
    const stored = localStorage.getItem(CART_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!stored) return [];

    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(stored);
    if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return [];

    return parsed.filter(isValidCartItem);
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export const CartProvider: React.FC<{ children: ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<CustomOrderCartItem[]>(loadCartFromStorage);
  const [isCartOpen, setIsCartOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(CART_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(items));
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Could not save cart to localStorage', e);
    }
  }, [items]);

  const addCustomOrderItem = (item: Omit<CustomOrderCartItem, 'id'>) => {
    const newItem: CustomOrderCartItem = {
      ...item,
      id: `order-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
    };
    setItems((prev) => [...prev, newItem]);
    setIsCartOpen(true);
  };

  const removeFromCart = (itemId: string) => {
    setItems((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== itemId));
  };

  const updateQuantity = (itemId: string, quantity: number) => {
    if (quantity <= 0) {
      removeFromCart(itemId);
      return;
    }
    setItems((prev) =>
      prev.map((item) => (item.id === itemId ? { ...item, quantity } : item))
    );
  };

  const clearCart = () => setItems([]);

  const totalCount = items.reduce((acc, item) => acc + item.quantity, 0);
  const totalPrice = items.reduce((acc, item) => acc + item.unitPrice * item.quantity, 0);

  const openCart = () => setIsCartOpen(true);
  const closeCart = () => setIsCartOpen(false);

  return (
    <CartContext.Provider
      value={{
        items,
        addCustomOrderItem,
        removeFromCart,
        updateQuantity,
        clearCart,
        totalCount,
        totalPrice,
        isCartOpen,
        openCart,
        closeCart,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </CartContext.Provider>
  );
};

export function useCart(): CartContextType {
  const context = useContext(CartContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useCart must be used within a CartProvider');
  }
  return context;
}
================================================================
FILE: src/context/LanguageContext.tsx
================================================================
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Language, translations, TranslationsType } from '../data/translations';

interface LanguageContextType {
  language: Language;
  setLanguage: (lang: Language) => void;
  t: TranslationsType;
}

const LanguageContext = createContext<LanguageContextType | undefined>(undefined);

const STORAGE_KEY = 'jardin_language';
const DEFAULT_LANGUAGE: Language = 'ru';

export const LanguageProvider: React.FC<{ children: ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [language, setLanguageState] = useState<Language>(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) as Language | null;
      if (saved && (saved === 'ru' || saved === 'en' || saved === 'es')) {
        return saved;
      }
    } catch {
      // fallback
    }
    return DEFAULT_LANGUAGE;
  });

  const setLanguage = (lang: Language) => {
    setLanguageState(lang);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, lang);
      document.documentElement.lang = lang;
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.lang = language;
  }, [language]);

  const t = translations[language];

  return (
    <LanguageContext.Provider value={{ language, setLanguage, t }}>
      {children}
    </LanguageContext.Provider>
  );
};

export function useLanguage(): LanguageContextType {
  const context = useContext(LanguageContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useLanguage must be used within a LanguageProvider');
  }
  return context;
}

================================================================
FILE: src/context/ModalContext.tsx
================================================================
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, ReactNode } from 'react';
import { GalleryItem, Product } from '../types';

interface ModalContextType {
  activePhoto: GalleryItem | null;
  openPhotoModal: (item: GalleryItem) => void;
  closePhotoModal: () => void;

  isRequestModalOpen: boolean;
  requestModalTitle: string;
  openRequestModal: (title?: string) => void;
  closeRequestModal: () => void;

  isContactChoiceOpen: boolean;
  contactChoiceData: { title?: string; onSelectMethod?: (method: string) => void } | null;
  openContactChoiceModal: (data?: { title?: string; onSelectMethod?: (method: string) => void }) => void;
  closeContactChoiceModal: () => void;

  quickViewProduct: Product | null;
  openQuickView: (product: Product) => void;
  closeQuickView: () => void;
}

const ModalContext = createContext<ModalContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export const ModalProvider: React.FC<{ children: ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [activePhoto, setActivePhoto] = useState<GalleryItem | null>(null);
  const [isRequestModalOpen, setIsRequestModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [requestModalTitle, setRequestModalTitle] = useState('');
  const [isContactChoiceOpen, setIsContactChoiceOpen] = useState(false);
  const [contactChoiceData, setContactChoiceData] = useState<{
    title?: string;
    onSelectMethod?: (method: string) => void;
  } | null>(null);
  const [quickViewProduct, setQuickViewProduct] = useState<Product | null>(null);

  const openPhotoModal = (item: GalleryItem) => setActivePhoto(item);
  const closePhotoModal = () => setActivePhoto(null);

  const openRequestModal = (title?: string) => {
    setRequestModalTitle(title || '');
    setIsRequestModalOpen(true);
  };
  const closeRequestModal = () => {
    setIsRequestModalOpen(false);
    setRequestModalTitle('');
  };

  const openContactChoiceModal = (data?: { title?: string; onSelectMethod?: (method: string) => void }) => {
    setContactChoiceData(data || null);
    setIsContactChoiceOpen(true);
  };
  const closeContactChoiceModal = () => {
    setIsContactChoiceOpen(false);
    setContactChoiceData(null);
  };

  const openQuickView = (product: Product) => setQuickViewProduct(product);
  const closeQuickView = () => setQuickViewProduct(null);

  return (
    <ModalContext.Provider
      value={{
        activePhoto,
        openPhotoModal,
        closePhotoModal,
        isRequestModalOpen,
        requestModalTitle,
        openRequestModal,
        closeRequestModal,
        isContactChoiceOpen,
        contactChoiceData,
        openContactChoiceModal,
        closeContactChoiceModal,
        quickViewProduct,
        openQuickView,
        closeQuickView,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </ModalContext.Provider>
  );
};

export function useModal(): ModalContextType {
  const context = useContext(ModalContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useModal must be used within a ModalProvider');
  }
  return context;
}

FILE: src/data/translations/index.ts

import { ru } from './ru';
import { en } from './en';
import { es } from './es';

export type Language = 'ru' | 'en' | 'es';
export const translations = { ru, en, es };
export type TranslationKey = keyof typeof ru;
export type TranslationsType = typeof ru;

================================================================

FILE: src/hooks/useDocumentTitle.ts
================================================================
import { useEffect } from 'react';

export function useDocumentTitle(title: string): void {
  useEffect(() => {
    const originalTitle = document.title;
    document.title = title ? `${title} | Jardin` : 'Jardin — Постижерное ателье';

    return () => {
      document.title = originalTitle;
    };
  }, [title]);
}

================================================================
FILE: src/hooks/useLazyVisible.tsx
================================================================
import { useState, useEffect, useRef, RefObject } from 'react';

export function useLazyVisible<T extends HTMLElement = HTMLDivElement>(
  threshold: number = 0.15,
  triggerOnce: boolean = true
): [RefObject<T>, boolean] {
  const ref = useRef<T>(null);
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const element = ref.current;
    if (!element) return;

    if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) {
      setIsVisible(true);
      return;
    }

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      ([entry]) => {
        if (entry.isIntersecting) {
          setIsVisible(true);
          if (triggerOnce) {
            observer.unobserve(entry.target);
          }
        } else if (!triggerOnce) {
          setIsVisible(false);
        }
      },
      {
        threshold,
        rootMargin: '50px',
      }
    );

    observer.observe(element);

    return () => {
      observer.disconnect();
    };
  }, [threshold, triggerOnce]);

  return [ref, isVisible];
}

================================================================
FILE: src/hooks/useLocalStorage.ts
================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

export function useLocalStorage<T>(key: string, initialValue: T): [T, (value: T | ((val: T) => T)) => void] {
  const [storedValue, setStoredValue] = useState<T>(() => {
    try {
      const item = window.localStorage.getItem(key);
      return item ? (JSON.parse(item) as T) : initialValue;
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn(`Error reading localStorage key "${key}":`, error);
      return initialValue;
    }
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(storedValue));
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn(`Error writing to localStorage key "${key}":`, error);
    }
  }, [key, storedValue]);

  return [storedValue, setStoredValue];
}

import React from 'react';
import './AboutPage.css';
import { PageType } from '../../types';
import { useDocumentTitle } from '../../hooks/useDocumentTitle';
import { useLanguage } from '../../context/LanguageContext';

export interface AboutPageProps {
  onNavigate: (page: PageType) => void;
}

export const AboutPage: React.FC<AboutPageProps> = ({ onNavigate }) => {
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  useDocumentTitle(t.nav.about);

  return (
    <div className="about-page fade-in">
      {/* ============================================
          HERO + ОСНОВНОЙ КОНТЕНТ — одна секция
          ============================================ */}
      <section className="section about-main-section">
        <div className="container">
          {/* Hero — заголовок страницы */}
          <div className="about-hero">
            <SectionHeader
              badge="Философия мастерства"
              title="О бренде и мастере Jardin"
              subtitle="Постижерное искусство высшей категории: создание изделий, неотличимых от собственных волос"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Текст + карточка стандартов */}
          <div className="about-content-grid">
            <div className="about-text-block">
              <h3 className="about-text-block__heading">
                Истинный люкс начинается с невидимых деталей
              </h3>
              <p>
                Ателье «Jardin» — это авторская мастерская, где каждая работа создаётся
                вручную, без спешки и шаблонов. Мы убеждены, что парик или накладка должны
                не просто дополнять образ, а дарить ощущение свободы и внутреннего
                спокойствия — как у своих родных волос.
              </p>
              <p>
                Каждая прядь отбирается вручную: мы работаем только с натуральными волосами
                премиального качества Remy — мягкими, шелковистыми, живыми. Это значит,
                что изделие сохранит естественную кутикулу, блеск и объём на долгие годы
                даже при регулярной носке.
              </p>
              <p>
                Каждый волос вплетается вручную — специальным постижерным крючком, слой за
                слоем, кропотливо и без права на ошибку. Именно из этой внимательности к
                деталям рождается иллюзия естественности: невидимый пробор, точный угол
                роста волос, аккуратная посадка по форме головы.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="about-image-card">
              <h4 className="about-image-card__title">
                Стандарты ателье Jardin
              </h4>
              <div className="about-image-card__list">
                <div className="about-image-card__item">
                  <strong>100% Remy Virgin Hair</strong> — никакого силикона, кислотной
                  обработки или синтетических примесей.
                </div>
                <div className="about-image-card__item">
                  <strong>Анатомический каркас</strong> — точное повторение формы черепа и
                  краевой линии роста.
                </div>
                <div className="about-image-card__item">
                  <strong>Шелковый пробор (Silk Top)</strong> — отсутствие видимых узелков
                  даже при макросъемке.
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* ============================================
          ЦЕННОСТИ
          ============================================ */}
      <section className="section about-values-section">
        <div className="container">
          <div className="about-values-list">
            <div className="about-value-item">
              <h4>Деликатность</h4>
              <p>Полная конфиденциальность каждого обращения, примерки в приватном пространстве в Валенсии.</p>
            </div>
            <div className="about-value-item">
              <h4>Перфекционизм</h4>
              <p>Сотни часов ручной ювелирной работы ради одной цели — естественности, в которую верят безоговорочно.</p>
            </div>
            <div className="about-value-item">
              <h4>Забота и сервис</h4>
              <p>Бесплатное сервисное обслуживание, адаптация длины и профессиональная поддержка по уходу.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* ============================================
          ПЛЕЙСХОЛДЕР — мастерская
          ============================================ */}
      <section className="section about-showcase-section">
        <div className="container">
          <SectionHeader
            badge="Атмосфера ателье"
            title="Мастерская Jardin"
            subtitle="Скоро здесь появятся фотографии процесса, видео работы мастера и другие детали ателье"
          />

          <div className="about-showcase__grid">
            <div className="about-showcase__placeholder">
              <span>Фото мастерской</span>
            </div>
            <div className="about-showcase__placeholder">
              <span>Видео процесса</span>
            </div>
            <div className="about-showcase__placeholder">
              <span>Детали работы</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* ============================================
          CTA
          ============================================ */}
      <CTABanner
        onOrderClick={() => onNavigate('calculator')}
        onContactClick={() => onNavigate('contacts')}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src/pages/ContactsPage/ContactsPage.tsx
================================================================
// src/pages/ContactsPage/ContactsPage.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import './ContactsPage.css';
import { PageType } from '../../types';
import { useDocumentTitle } from '../../hooks/useDocumentTitle';
import { useLanguage } from '../../context/LanguageContext';
import { contactInfo } from '../../data/contacts';
import { SectionHeader } from '../../components/ui/SectionHeader';
import { Button } from '../../components/ui/Button';
import { Icon } from '../../components/ui/Icon';
import { formatPhone, isValidPhone } from '../../utils/formatPhone';
import { sendRequest } from '../../utils/sendRequest';

export interface ContactsPageProps {
  onNavigate: (page: PageType) => void;
}

export const ContactsPage: React.FC<ContactsPageProps> = ({ onNavigate }) => {
  const { language, t } = useLanguage();
  useDocumentTitle(t.nav.contacts);

  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
  const [agreed, setAgreed] = useState(false);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [isSuccess, setIsSuccess] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState('');

  const handlePhoneChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    setPhone(formatPhone(e.target.value));
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!name.trim()) {
      setErrorMsg(t.errors.requiredName);
      return;
    }
    if (!isValidPhone(phone)) {
      setErrorMsg(t.errors.invalidPhone);
      return;
    }
    if (!agreed) {
      setErrorMsg(t.errors.privacyRequired);
      return;
    }

    setErrorMsg('');
    setIsSubmitting(true);

    const res = await sendRequest({
      name,
      phone,
      email,
      message,
      preferredMethod: 'Форма контактов',
    });

    setIsSubmitting(false);
    if (res.success) {
      setIsSuccess(true);
      setName('');
      setPhone('');
      setEmail('');
      setMessage('');
      setAgreed(false);
    } else {
      setErrorMsg(t.errors.generalError);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="contacts-page fade-in">
      <div className="container">
        <SectionHeader
          badge={t.contacts.badge}
          title={t.contacts.title}
          subtitle={t.contacts.subtitle}
        />

        <div className="contacts-grid">
          {/* LEFT COLUMN — Direct Info */}
          <div className="contacts-info-card">
            <div className="contacts-item">
              <div className="contacts-item__icon">
                <Icon name="mapPin" size={20} />
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="contacts-item__label">{t.contacts.addressTitle}</div>
                <div className="contacts-item__value">{contactInfo.address[language]}</div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="contacts-item">
              <div className="contacts-item__icon">
                <Icon name="phone" size={20} />
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="contacts-item__label">{t.contacts.phoneTitle}</div>
                <div className="contacts-item__value">
                  <a href={`tel:${contactInfo.phoneRaw}`} className="contacts-item__link contacts-item__link--gold">
                    {contactInfo.phone}
                  </a>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="contacts-item">
              <div className="contacts-item__icon">
                <Icon name="email" size={20} />
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="contacts-item__label">{t.contacts.emailTitle}</div>
                <div className="contacts-item__value">
                  <a href={`mailto:${contactInfo.email}`} className="contacts-item__link">
                    {contactInfo.email}
                  </a>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="contacts-item">
              <div className="contacts-item__icon">
                <Icon name="clock" size={20} />
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="contacts-item__label">{t.contacts.hoursTitle}</div>
                <div className="contacts-item__value">{contactInfo.workingHours[language]}</div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Messengers */}
            <div className="contacts-messengers">
              <div className="contacts-item__label contacts-messengers__title">
                {t.contacts.messengersTitle}
              </div>
              <div className="contacts-messengers__row">
                <a
                  href={contactInfo.telegram}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="contacts-messenger-btn"
                >
                  <Icon name="telegram" size={16} />
                  <span>Telegram</span>
                </a>
                <a
                  href={contactInfo.whatsapp}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="contacts-messenger-btn"
                >
                  <Icon name="whatsapp" size={16} />
                  <span>WhatsApp</span>
                </a>
                <a
                  href={contactInfo.instagram}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="contacts-messenger-btn"
                >
                  <Icon name="instagram" size={16} />
                  <span>Instagram</span>
                </a>
                <a
                  href={contactInfo.tiktok}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="contacts-messenger-btn"
                >
                  <Icon name="tiktok" size={16} />
                  <span>TikTok</span>
                </a>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* RIGHT COLUMN — Interactive Form */}
          <div className="contacts-form-card">
            {isSuccess ? (
              <div className="contacts-success">
                <Icon
                  name="check"
                  size={54}
                  className="contacts-success__icon"
                />
                <h3 className="contacts-success__title">
                  {t.customOrder.successTitle}
                </h3>
                <p className="contacts-success__text">
                  {t.customOrder.successDesc}
                </p>
                <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => setIsSuccess(false)}>
                  Отправить еще сообщение
                </Button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="contacts-form">
                <div>
                  <h3 className="contacts-form__title">{t.contacts.formTitle}</h3>
                  <p className="contacts-form__subtitle">{t.contacts.formSubtitle}</p>
                </div>

                <div className="contacts-form__group">
                  <label className="contacts-form__label">
                    {t.forms.name} *
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={name}
                    onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder={t.forms.namePlaceholder}
                    className="contacts-form__input"
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="contacts-form__group">
                  <label className="contacts-form__label">
                    {t.forms.phone} *
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="tel"
                    required
                    value={phone}
                    onChange={handlePhoneChange}
                    placeholder={t.forms.phonePlaceholder}
                    className="contacts-form__input"
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="contacts-form__group">
                  <label className="contacts-form__label">
                    {t.forms.email}
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder={t.forms.emailPlaceholder}
                    className="contacts-form__input"
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="contacts-form__group">
                  <label className="contacts-form__label">
                    {t.forms.message}
                  </label>
                  <textarea
                    rows={3}
                    value={message}
                    onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder={t.forms.messagePlaceholder}
                    className="contacts-form__input contacts-form__textarea"
                  />
                </div>

                <label className="contacts-form__agreement">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    required
                    checked={agreed}
                    onChange={(e) => setAgreed(e.target.checked)}
                    className="contacts-form__checkbox"
                  />
                  <span>
                    {t.customOrder.privacyNotice}{' '}
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={(e) => {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        onNavigate('privacy');
                      }}
                      className="contacts-form__privacy-link"
                    >
                      {t.customOrder.privacyLink}
                    </button>
                  </span>
                </label>

                {errorMsg && (
                  <div className="contacts-form__error">{errorMsg}</div>
                )}

                <Button type="submit" variant="primary" size="lg" disabled={isSubmitting}>
                  {isSubmitting ? t.customOrder.sending : t.forms.send}
                </Button>
              </form>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// src/pages/HomePage/HomePage.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { PageType } from '../../types';
import { useDocumentTitle } from '../../hooks/useDocumentTitle';
import { useLanguage } from '../../context/LanguageContext';
import { useModal } from '../../context/ModalContext';
import { Hero } from '../../components/sections/Hero';
import { AdvantagesSection } from '../../components/sections/Advantages';
import { CalculatorTeaser } from '../../components/sections/CalculatorTeaser';
import { GallerySection } from '../../components/sections/Gallery';
import { CTABanner } from '../../components/sections/CTA';

export interface HomePageProps {
  onNavigate: (page: PageType) => void;
}

export const HomePage: React.FC<HomePageProps> = ({ onNavigate }) => {
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  useDocumentTitle(t.nav.home);
  const { openRequestModal } = useModal();

  return (
    <div className="home-page fade-in">
      <Hero
        onOrderClick={() => onNavigate('calculator')}
        onShopClick={() => onNavigate('gallery')}
      />

      <AdvantagesSection />

      <CalculatorTeaser onOpenCalculator={() => onNavigate('calculator')} />

      <GallerySection isPreview onViewAllClick={() => onNavigate('gallery')} />

      <CTABanner
        onOrderClick={() => onNavigate('calculator')}
        onContactClick={() => openRequestModal(t.contacts.formTitle)}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

// src/pages/PrivacyPolicyPage/PrivacyPolicyPage.tsx

import React from 'react';
import './PrivacyPolicyPage.css';
import { PageType } from '../../types';
import { useDocumentTitle } from '../../hooks/useDocumentTitle';
import { useLanguage } from '../../context/LanguageContext';
import { Button } from '../../components/ui/Button';

export interface PrivacyPolicyPageProps {
  onNavigate: (page: PageType) => void;
}

export const PrivacyPolicyPage: React.FC<PrivacyPolicyPageProps> = ({ onNavigate }) => {
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  useDocumentTitle(t.privacy.title);

  return (
    <div className="privacy-page fade-in">
      <div className="container privacy-container">
        <article className="privacy-parchment">
          {/* Декоративные уголки — «бумажный холст» */}
          <span className="privacy-parchment__corner privacy-parchment__corner--tl" aria-hidden />
          <span className="privacy-parchment__corner privacy-parchment__corner--tr" aria-hidden />
          <span className="privacy-parchment__corner privacy-parchment__corner--bl" aria-hidden />
          <span className="privacy-parchment__corner privacy-parchment__corner--br" aria-hidden />

          {/* Заголовок — рукописный */}
          <header className="privacy-parchment__header">
            <h1 className="privacy-parchment__title">{t.privacy.title}</h1>
            <span className="privacy-parchment__date">{t.privacy.lastUpdated}</span>
            <div className="privacy-parchment__divider" aria-hidden>
              <span className="privacy-parchment__divider-dot" />
              <span className="privacy-parchment__divider-line" />
              <span className="privacy-parchment__divider-dot" />
            </div>
          </header>

          {/* Вступление */}
          <p className="privacy-parchment__intro">{t.privacy.intro}</p>

          {/* Разделы */}
          <section className="privacy-parchment__block">
            <h2 className="privacy-parchment__section-title">{t.privacy.section1Title}</h2>
            <p>{t.privacy.section1Text}</p>
          </section>

          <section className="privacy-parchment__block">
            <h2 className="privacy-parchment__section-title">{t.privacy.section2Title}</h2>
            <p>{t.privacy.section2Text}</p>
          </section>

          <section className="privacy-parchment__block">
            <h2 className="privacy-parchment__section-title">{t.privacy.section3Title}</h2>
            <p>{t.privacy.section3Text}</p>
          </section>

          <section className="privacy-parchment__block">
            <h2 className="privacy-parchment__section-title">{t.privacy.section4Title}</h2>
            <p>{t.privacy.section4Text}</p>
          </section>

          {/* Подпись + кнопка */}
          <footer className="privacy-parchment__footer">
            <div className="privacy-parchment__signature" aria-hidden>
              <span className="privacy-parchment__signature-flourish">~ Jardin Atelier ~</span>
            </div>

            <div className="privacy-parchment__back-btn">
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                onClick={() => onNavigate('home')}
              >
                {t.privacy.backHome}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </footer>
        </article>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// src/types/index.ts

export type Language = 'ru' | 'en' | 'es';

export interface LocalizedText {
  ru: string;
  en: string;
  es: string;
}

export type ProductCategory = 'wigs' | 'toppers' | 'wefts' | 'bangs' | 'strands';

export interface Product {
  id: string;
  name: LocalizedText;
  category: ProductCategory;
  description: LocalizedText;
  price: number;
  image: string;
  hairType: LocalizedText;
  lengthCm: number;
  density?: string;
  isPopular?: boolean;
  inStock: boolean;
}

export interface HairColor {
  id: string;
  name: LocalizedText;
  hex: string;
  priceMultiplier: number;
  toneCategory: 'blonde' | 'brown' | 'dark' | 'copper' | 'grey';
}

export interface ProductShape {
  id: 'wig' | 'topper' | 'bangs' | 'strands' | 'wefts';
  name: LocalizedText;
  priceMultiplier: number;
  basePrice: number;
  description: LocalizedText;
}

export interface LengthOption {
  lengthCm: number;
  priceMultiplier: number;
}

/**
 * Позиция индивидуального заказа в корзине.
 * Каждая позиция — уникальная конфигурация (форма + длина + цвет + количество).
 */
export interface CustomOrderCartItem {
  id: string;
  shapeId: 'wig' | 'topper' | 'bangs' | 'strands' | 'wefts';
  shapeName: LocalizedText;
  lengthCm: number;
  colorId: string;
  colorName: LocalizedText;
  colorHex: string;
  quantity: number;
  unitPrice: number;
}

export interface CustomOrderRequest {
  name: string;
  phone: string;
  email: string;
  shape: string;
  lengthCm: number;
  colorId: string;
  colorName: string;
  calculatedPrice: number;
  comment?: string;
  contactMethod?: 'telegram' | 'whatsapp' | 'phone' | 'email';
  agreedToPrivacy: boolean;
}

export interface GalleryItem {
  id: string;
  imageUrl: string;
  title: LocalizedText;
  category: string;
  description: LocalizedText;
}

export interface Advantage {
  id: string;
  title: LocalizedText;
  description: LocalizedText;
  icon: string;
}

export interface ContactInfo {
  phone: string;
  phoneRaw: string;
  email: string;
  address: LocalizedText;
  workingHours: LocalizedText;
  telegram: string;
  whatsapp: string;
  instagram: string;
  tiktok: string;
}

export type PageType = 'home' | 'gallery' | 'about' | 'contacts' | 'privacy' | 'calculator';

FILE: src/utils/cn.ts

export function cn(...classes: (string | boolean | undefined | null)[]): string {
  return classes.filter(Boolean).join(' ');
}

================================================================
FILE: src/utils/formatPhone.ts
================================================================
/**
 * Formats a raw phone string into a masked phone format: +7 (XXX) XXX-XX-XX
 */
export function formatPhone(value: string): string {
  if (!value) return '';

  // Extract only digits
  const cleaned = value.replace(/\D/g, '');

  if (cleaned.length === 0) return '';

  // Standardize starting digit to 7 if starts with 8 or 7
  let digits = cleaned;
  if (digits[0] === '8' || digits[0] === '7') {
    digits = digits.slice(1);
  }

  let result = '+7';

  if (digits.length > 0) {
    result += ` (${digits.substring(0, 3)}`;
  }
  if (digits.length >= 3) {
    result += `) ${digits.substring(3, 6)}`;
  }
  if (digits.length >= 6) {
    result += `-${digits.substring(6, 8)}`;
  }
  if (digits.length >= 8) {
    result += `-${digits.substring(8, 10)}`;
  }

  return result;
}

/**
 * Validates whether the phone number has enough digits (at least 10 digits without country code)
 */
export function isValidPhone(value: string): boolean {
  const digits = value.replace(/\D/g, '');
  return digits.length >= 11;
}

================================================================
FILE: src/utils/formatPrice.ts
================================================================
// src/utils/formatPrice.ts

/**
 * Форматирует число в читаемую цену с валютой.
 * Пример: 185000 -> "185 000 €"
 *
 * @param price — число
 * @param currency — символ валюты, по умолчанию евро
 * @param locale — 'ru' | 'en' | 'es' — влияет на формат разделителей
 */
export function formatPrice(
  price: number,
  currency: string = '€',
  locale: string = 'ru-RU'
): string {
  const intlLocale =
    locale === 'ru' ? 'ru-RU' :
      locale === 'es' ? 'es-ES' :
        locale === 'en' ? 'en-GB' :   // для евро лучше en-GB (европейский формат), чем en-US
          'ru-RU';

  const formatted = new Intl.NumberFormat(intlLocale, {
    maximumFractionDigits: 0,
  }).format(price);

  return `${formatted} ${currency}`;
}

FILE: src/utils/sendRequest.ts

// src/utils/sendRequest.ts

import { CustomOrderRequest } from '../types';
import { sendTelegramMessage } from './sendTelegramMessage';

export interface FeedbackRequestData {
  name: string;
  phone: string;
  email?: string;
  message?: string;
  preferredMethod?: string;
}

export async function sendRequest(
  data: CustomOrderRequest | FeedbackRequestData
): Promise<{ success: boolean; message?: string }> {
  try {
    let messageText = '';

    if ('shape' in data) {
      // Custom Order Request
      messageText =
        `✨ <b>НОВАЯ ЗАЯВКА НА ПОШИВ (Jardin Atelier)</b>\n\n` +
        `👤 <b>Клиент:</b> ${data.name}\n` +
        `📞 <b>Телефон:</b> ${data.phone}\n` +
        `✉️ <b>Email:</b> ${data.email || 'Не указан'}\n` +
        `💇‍♀️ <b>Форма:</b> ${data.shape}\n` +
        `📏 <b>Длина:</b> ${data.lengthCm} см\n` +
        `🎨 <b>Оттенок:</b> ${data.colorName} (${data.colorId})\n` +
        `💰 <b>Расчетная сумма:</b> ${data.calculatedPrice.toLocaleString('ru-RU')} €\n` +
        `💬 <b>Способ связи:</b> ${data.contactMethod || 'Любой'}\n` +
        (data.comment ? `📝 <b>Комментарий:</b> ${data.comment}\n` : '');
    } else {
      // General feedback inquiry
      messageText =
        `📩 <b>НОВОЕ ОБРАЩЕНИЕ С САЙТА (Jardin)</b>\n\n` +
        `👤 <b>Имя:</b> ${data.name}\n` +
        `📞 <b>Телефон:</b> ${data.phone}\n` +
        `✉️ <b>Email:</b> ${data.email || 'Не указан'}\n` +
        (data.preferredMethod ? `💬 <b>Способ связи:</b> ${data.preferredMethod}\n` : '') +
        (data.message ? `📝 <b>Сообщение:</b> ${data.message}\n` : '');
    }

    // Save locally for persistence / demonstration
    try {
      const existingInquiries = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('jardin_inquiries') || '[]');
      existingInquiries.push({ ...data, createdAt: new Date().toISOString() });
      localStorage.setItem('jardin_inquiries', JSON.stringify(existingInquiries));
    } catch {
      // ignore localStorage errors
    }

    // Send Telegram Notification
    await sendTelegramMessage(messageText);

    // Simulate short network latency for realism
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 600));

    return { success: true };
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('sendRequest error:', err);
    return {
      success: false,
      message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown error',
    };
  }
}
================================================================
FILE: src/utils/sendTelegramMessage.ts
================================================================
/**
 * Sends a notification message to a Telegram chat using Telegram Bot API
 */
export async function sendTelegramMessage(text: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const botToken = import.meta.env.VITE_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN;
  const chatId = import.meta.env.VITE_TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID;

  if (!botToken || !chatId) {
    console.info('Telegram credentials not configured in env (VITE_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN / VITE_TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID). Simulating dispatch:');
    console.info(text);
    return true;
  }

  try {
    const url = `https://api.telegram.org/bot${botToken}/sendMessage`;
    const response = await fetch(url, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        chat_id: chatId,
        text,
        parse_mode: 'HTML',
      }),
    });

    return response.ok;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Failed to send Telegram message:', error);
    return false;
  }
}
